Every recommendation, every store, April 2026

The engine ran once per trading day at five stores, exactly as production does. Each card it issued is below with what actually happened to it at 30, 60, 90 and 120 days — which, for a month of April cards, means outcomes measured in May, June, July and August.

531 cards · 122 store-products · 138 store-days · scored at four horizons · All 5 stores carry usable cost data, so every money figure on this page is measured rather than assumed.

net margin, May
margin captured less the cost of holding what did not sell, on 527 priced cards
5
stores measured
judged one store at a time; the engine is switched on one store at a time

How to read a hit

A card is a hit when the product sold more over the window than its own trailing rate implied it would — a lift of 1.00× or better against the baseline the card itself published. It is not "the store ordered it" and not "the product sold something". The baseline is the SKU's own recent run-rate over open days, so a product that was always going to sell is not credited to the engine.

A card scored at four horizons is one decision watched for four months, not four decisions. The engine is not re-run per horizon; re-running it would be a different store on a different day.

00The comparison — five stores, four horizons

The only table in the audit that asks the same question of every store at every horizon. A rate without its denominator is not shown: 100% of two cards is not a result.

Generated by scripts/build_combined_ledger.py from the same ledger.json files the per-store pages are built from, so a figure here and a figure there cannot disagree. Opens from a file:// URL with no network: no CDN, no fonts, no script tags.

What this page does and does not measure. It measures the sheet-20 reorder path — temporal reversion, the materiality and confidence gates, the merchandise screen, the calibrated confidence, the track-record screen and the published interval. It does not measure the live route's stock families (the export carries no on-hand, so there are no stockout or overstock cards here), the signal families, or the slate's cash budget. Those are live on the serving path and are reported in the engine handover, not here.
